# Yames tools: parity inventory for the learning-path revamp

**Status:** read-only audit, 2026-11-08. **Baseline:** `/home/turutupa/Dev/yames-merge`, branch `songs-v1`, commit `d7d09b0f`, version 1.2.2 (includes main). No repo file was changed, nothing was installed, and the app and tests were not run.

**What this is:** the merge condition for the revamp. The owner's rule is "the tools should not lose any of their functionality". Every row is a capability that must still exist afterwards. A row that cannot be ticked is a bug or a decision, never a row to delete quietly.

**How to read a row.** ID; what the user can do; every control or input path; code location; test today; saved state. Saved state can be a key in `settings.json`, a field in a saved record, a SQLite table, or a file on disk. Line references are for commit `d7d09b0f`. A bare `:123` in a Code cell means the same file as the previous reference in that cell.

**Rows that state an absence** (for example SET-60 no setlist import or export, PRS-15 no preset reordering, SYS-09 no URL deep links) are there so nobody assumes a capability exists, and so a new feature is not mistaken for parity.

**Evidence levels.** Rows were written by reading code, one pass per area. The checks in section 1 were run by script over the final tables. Claims marked **[V]** in sections 3 to 6 were re-checked by hand against the code. Everything else is a code reading that was not re-checked and has never been run.

## Contents

1. Completeness proof
2. Inventory: 2.1 Metronome, 2.2 Ramp up, 2.3 Setlist, 2.4 Jam, 2.5 Songs, 2.6 Practice Coach, 2.7 Cross-cutting
3. Hard to keep in a one-tap UI
4. Must not change
5. Automated coverage and the smallest parity suite
6. Discrepancies
7. Appendix A: every command and event mapped to IDs. Appendix B: supporting source files with no row of their own

| **Total capability rows** | | **969** | **271** |

Checks below were run by script over these tables. Coverage means the exact name occurs in at least one row, whose code citation was written by the area pass; it does not mean the row was verified at runtime.

| Enumerated from code | How | Count | Not covered |
|---|---|---:|---|
| Tauri commands, registered | `generate_handler!` in src-tauri/src/lib.rs | 144 | 0 |
| Commands invoked from src/ipc.ts | regex over `invoke(`, as src/ipc.commands.test.ts does | 142 | 0 |
| Events | `listen(` in src/ipc.ts, and `.emit(` in src-tauri/src | 29 | 0 |
| Hotkey actions | `HOTKEYS` in src/hotkeys.ts | 42 | 0 |
| MIDI actions | same ids: `midiBindings.action` is any `HOTKEYS` id, learned from the Hotkeys table | 42 | 0 |
| Settings and store keys | all `storeSave`, `storeLoad`, Rust `store.get/set`, plus `songs.json` and `localStorage`; dynamic keys counted once | 92 | 0 |
| SQLite tables | `CREATE TABLE` in src-tauri/src/db.rs | 12 | 0 |
| Rail modes | `PLAY_TABS`, `MODES` in Rail.tsx | 5 | 0 |
| Theme ids | src/themes.ts | 13 | 0 |
| Sound set ids | `SOUND_TYPES` | 8 | 0 |
| Languages | directories in src/locales | 15 | 0 |
| Zen styles | src/containers/zen | 7 | 0 |
| Meter choices | `METER_PRESETS` plus FREE | 10 | 0 |
| Tour stops | src/containers/onboarding/tour/stops.ts | 6 | 0 |
| Hint ids | `HINT_IDS` | 9 | 0 |

**Code reference check.** All 3,200+ file references resolve to a real file, and no line number is past the end of its file. A random sample of 28 rows was read by hand against the first cited line: most landed on the named code, two landed nearby rather than on it (`COA-94`, `SNR-96`). Treat a line number as an entry point, not an exact address. Of 180 citations in the form `path:line` followed by a named symbol, 176 had the name within six lines; the other 4 are mostly the script misreading shorthand, and were not re-checked. Other bare `:N` references were not checked by script.

**Test name check.** All 1,043 cited test names were searched in the test sources. 19 did not match literally: template-literal names abbreviated with `...`, names with escaped quotes, and a few where the cited title was shortened. These are in SNR-*, ONB-*, MET-50. Treat those as approximate names.

**Other counts.** English locale: 36 groups, 1910 keys, used as a checklist by each pass but not mapped key by key. Tests: 226 Vitest files, 19 Playwright specs, 980 `#[test]` in Rust (grep count; none were run). Source file check: 380 non-test source files (excluding barrels, dev binaries, mocks and locales), 39 not cited by any row, each accounted for in Appendix B.

**Not statically checkable.** Anything audible or timed; whether a control is actually reachable at every window size; real devices (MIDI, gamepad, camera, microphone, output pairs); OS integration (file open, tray, updater, window behaviour); coach model output; macOS and Linux behaviour; settings stored in real users' files; and whether a capability works as opposed to exists. Dynamic i18n keys and the `settings.*` sub-keys were read, not proven one by one.

**IPC with no frontend caller.** 11 `src/ipc.ts` wrappers have no non-test caller, so their capability has no route for a user today: `setCalibrationOffset`, `getCalibrationOffset`, `listCalibrationCache`, `reorderPresets`, `listSessionLogs`, `getSessionLog`, `clearSessionLogs`, `writeModelChunk`, `getModelsPath`, `downloadModelFile`, `startVoiceRepair`. They still have rows, so nothing is deleted by accident. Two commands are invoked outside `src/ipc.ts`: `app_ready` (src/App.tsx:20) and `save_window_position` (src/hooks/useDrag.ts:150). `fullscreen-changed` is listened for and never emitted; `onset-detected` is emitted and never heard.

#### 2.7.10 Calibration

| CAL-01 | Latency offset is learned automatically: after the analyzer's ~8-beat buffer fills with real samples it fires once per session and the (instrument, input device) offset is cached (confidence 1.0; only entries >= 0.95 are written to disk). ## 3. Hard to keep in a one-tap UI

What a low-friction front door is most likely to hide or break by accident. The rule behind every item: **the tool stays reachable under Tools, unchanged, and a coach-built Session drives the tool's existing code path instead of a parallel one.**

### 3.1 The ones most likely to be lost

| # | At risk | Why a Today/Session door breaks it | IDs | How to preserve it |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Stopping playback when the mode changes | Mode change stops the click and any ramp only inside `setView`. A new surface that bypasses it leaves two things playing | SHL-04, SHL-05 | Today and Session become `setView` targets. Keep Settings as a layer that returns to the covered mode |
| 2 | The window showing at all | The main window starts hidden until `app_ready` is called. Also `activeTab` only restores values in `PLAY_TABS` | SYS-17, SHL-07 | Call `app_ready` once from the new shell. Add new tabs to `PLAY_TABS`; never rename `beat` |
| 3 | First-run detection | Existing users are recognised by `instrument`, sessions and presets. A migration that drops or delays these opens the wizard over their data | ONB-01, ONB-02, COA-114 | Keep the detection inputs. Decide explicitly what an existing user sees on first Today |
| 4 | Scoring and history | A session records only through `start_evaluation`, stop before `get_final_session_report`, then `save_session`. A one-tap start that skips this produces no segment and no history | COA-07, COA-61 | Session runs through `useSession`; do not reimplement the order of calls |
| 5 | Song attempts and verdicts | A verdict needs the input on and a play-to-stop attempt with a minimum of 8 onsets. Restarting or re-pushing the schedule mid-pass corrupts it | SNR-05 | Keep the listen gate visible. Seek with `seek_song` rather than recompiling |
| 6 | Hands-free control | 42 actions by key, MIDI and gamepad, resolved per tab by `actionForCombo`. `C`, `R`, `L`, `[`, `]` mean different things per tab. `play` branches on `view`, and a view with no branch silently does nothing (this already shipped once on Songs) | HK-01 to HK-42, KEY-06 | Give each new view a `play` branch and a decision for every action. Run the HK table on each new view |
| 7 | Consent on recording | Takes and camera each have a one-time promise dialog, reached only through `requestTakes` and the camera `request` door | JTK-33, SNR-85 | One-tap recording goes through those doors, never `start_take` directly |
| 8 | Count-in | Separate state per mode. Space, widget and Zen play do not arm it on the Metronome; the on-screen Play does | MET-31 to MET-33, RMP-12 | Keep one control per mode. If a single Start appears, decide which behaviour it has and keep the switch honest |
| 9 | Unsaved edits | Setlists and jams are not auto-saved; loads go through the dirty guard | SET-61, SET-62, JTK-76, JTK-77 | Coach-loaded material must pass the guard or use throwaway copies |
| 10 | Per-song memory | Selection, loop, speed, mixer, count-in and portions live in `songMixes[songId]`. A part is its own record with its own history | SNG-27, SNR-96 | A path that opens a song restores it, or passes bars and tempo without overwriting it. Keep the part visible |
| 11 | Promises that change how a song opens | A "come back" promise reopens the song on that passage, and nothing ever clears it (section 6.1) | SNR-96, SNR-99 | Keep it, replace it, or fix clearing, but decide. Today's coach queue must not fight it |
| 12 | Settings-only routes | Update banner, Hotkeys and MIDI learn, input gain and channel, output pairs, Recalibrate, brain tier and voice, button flash, animations, language | SYS-03, KEY-01 to KEY-13, AUD-01 to AUD-22, CAL-04, COA-88, COA-102 to COA-113, STG-* | Settings stays, one tap from Today. The anchors `#settings-devices`, `#settings-coach`, `#settings-appearance` keep working |
| 13 | Exits to the old tools | Widget, Zen and OS file open assume a mode to return to. Zen has only three faces (`beat`, `drill`, `jam`). OS open forces Songs | WID-01, WID-02, ZEN-18, ZEN-19, SYS-07, SYS-08 | Decide what Zen and the widget show during a Session. OS open still lands in Songs |
| 14 | Quitting safely | Closing any window finishes a running take, saves positions and quits | SYS-19 | Background activity in a new shell must not bypass `CloseRequested` |
| 15 | Mid-Session tips | Tip buttons (drop or raise 10 BPM, rest, posture, retry calibration) exist only in the coach feed, as does the `?` shortcut | COA-06, COA-25, COA-26 | Hiding the feed removes them. Move them with the feed |
| 16 | History browsing | The coach History tab is the only per-session review surface besides the Metronome's last-session line | COA-44 to COA-48 | Keep it under the coach or under Progress, unchanged |

### 3.2 Deep controls per tool (keep under Tools, unchanged)

- **Metronome:** accent mode, quintuplets and sextuplets (no direct key), grouping variants, FREE mode, sticks and kit sounds, tap tempo (no key at all).
- **Ramp up:** per-phrase popovers for zigzag decrement, cyclic, adaptive aggressiveness and subdivision; click a climb cell to start from that bar. A "quick drill" must not drop these.
- **Setlist:** the four-way gap model (trigger manual, bars or seconds, crossed with transition cut, count-in or rest), repeat, top count-in. Skip exists only as a Transport button.
- **Jam:** 115 grooves and 24 progressions are reachable only through the Setup drawer; tapping a vibe overwrites tempo, key, form, band and more. Own kit folder, drawn grooves and pasted charts are power features that need a permanent route.
- **Songs:** five import paths; the Downloads offer fires only while the Songs tab is mounted, so a Today-first shell must keep it mounted or restart the watch. Exact bars, mixer, solo, drums choice and sound font within two taps.
- **Takes and video:** source picker, "Check the sound", nudge, and the Save as video options must stay; Jam deliberately has no bar range on export.

### 3.3 Notes per area, as found

**Metronome and Ramp up**

- The count-in switch is state-dependent per mode: one `warmupBeats` store serves Metronome and Drill, but jam, setlist and Songs use separate stores. Keep one Count-in control per mode, not a global one.
- Space, widget play and Zen play do not arm the count-in; only the on-screen Play button does. If the front door adds a single Start path it must pick one behaviour and make the switch honest.
- Adaptive needs the mic (Listens badge). A one-tap Start with input off will sit at the start tempo; keep the "Listens" cue and the "Input off" state visible.
- Accent mode, sticks/kit sound choice, quintuplet and sextuplet (no direct keys), and the grouping chips (5/4, 6/8, 7/8, 8/8 variants) are one click deep on the stage. Hiding the stage hides all of them; keep them reachable or give them a "more" drawer.
- Drill sub-settings live in per-phrase popovers (tempo, rate, repeats, beats, subdivision, sound, options). A "Quick drill" preset must still expose Zigzag decrement, cyclic, adaptive aggressiveness and subdivision.
- Click any climb cell starts the run from that bar (including during a run). A simplified drill view that drops the chart loses restart-from-step.
- `play` hotkey, `bpm-*` global (OS-level) shortcuts and MIDI actions for sub/sig must keep working on whatever replaces the Metronome tab.

**Setlist and presets**

- Setlist build is a four-way gap model (trigger manual/bars/seconds crossed with transition cut/count-in/rest, plus repeat and top count-in). A "one-tap play" front door must still expose a way to author these; preserve by keeping the Setlist editor (paragraph) reachable from any learning-path step that plays a routine.
- The run is armed and lands on bar lines; skip is the ONLY way past a "When I say" step and exists only as a Transport button (no hotkey, MIDI or widget path). Preserve the Transport skip and consider adding action ids.
- Jam steps depend on the jam library by id; deleting a jam silently degrades the step to a click. Preserve the "jam is gone" note and fallback fields.
- Setlist edits are not auto-saved (dirty/Revert/Save plus the unsaved gate). A front door that loads or switches objects without `guarded` would drop edits.
- Preset and setlist delete have no confirmation; preset context-menu (Update, Delete) is right-click only, with no keyboard or touch route.
- Presets cannot be added to setlists or reordered; any "build a routine from my presets" front door would be new functionality, not parity.
- Library hotkey `toggle-sidebar` does nothing on the Setlist tab; the empty-state hint tells users to press it.

**Jam, music**

- Tapping a vibe tile overwrites tempo, key, form, count-in, band, voices, kit, feel, intensity and fills and drops a drawn groove, custom kit, meter override and (if it will not fit) the typed progression. A front door that offers vibes must keep "refinements after the vibe" reachable (Setup sheet) and must not apply a tile over a tuned jam without a visible Revert (the existing dirty/Revert path covers this).
- The nine vibes x 32 variations, the 115 grooves in 9 families and the 24 named progressions are only reachable through the Setup drawer. Hiding the drawer hides the whole catalogue; keep a path from any quick start to "browse all grooves/progressions".
- Hands-free footswitch actions (`jam-next-groove`, `jam-prev-groove`, `jam-trade`, `jam-dropout`, `jam-next-shape`, `jam-next-section`, `jam-prev-section`, `jam-loop-section`) work only on the Jam tab with a jam loaded and stand aside while the groove editor is open. A learning-path player that wraps Jam must keep that same gating and bind-ability (Settings > Hotkeys).
- Practice tools (drop-out, trade, tempo trainer, chord-name switch) and section loop / jump are on the playing screen; a stripped-down player must keep them or the practice value of Jam is lost. The tempo-trainer climb is deliberately not saved.
- Song mode ends itself and emits `jam-ended`; any new player screen has to keep listening to it and stop the transport, or the next Play will count a stale count-in.
- Spoken cues depend on a global setting (`jamCues`) plus the coach voice; a low-friction front door should not hide the Settings toggle.
- Own kit folder (`pick_kit_folder`, `inspect_kit_folder`), drawn grooves (editor) and pasted charts are power features; they need a persistent route from the new front door, plus the rule that a vibe tile clears them.
- Meter override, transposition (Bb/Eb), key picker in two places and the pinned-shape corner are lightly used but each has a saved field; keep the fields round-tripping when the UI is restructured.

**Jam, takes and library**

- Recording is two nested opt-ins (takes per jam, camera per jam) with two one-time promise dialogs (`jam.takesIntroSeen`, `songs.cameraIntroSeen`): a front door that auto-records must still go through `requestTakes`/`request` so the dialogs show; never call `startTake` directly.
- "Everything this computer plays" is machine-wide and capability-probed (`check_take_sound`); hiding the source picker would silently pin `yamesAndInput`. Keep the picker plus the warning and "Check the sound" meter reachable.
- The Takes shelf lives only in the Set up sheet of the Jam tab; a learning-path flow that moves playing elsewhere must still show the take it just made and the 100 MB notice, and still end the take when the jam leaves the stage.
- Watch panel nudge, per-camera/per-source, is the only way to fix picture sync; it must stay reachable in any new review screen.
- Clip export is real-time (as long as the music) and Jam deliberately omits bar-range and marks; keep `canChooseBars=false`.
- Cheat sheet state (tab, scope, dots, expanded chord) is not remembered; pins/follow persist in the jam.
- Library behaviours with no button: seed once, restore `lastJamId`, dirty-guard on load/new, write-wins over initial read; a front door that creates throwaway jams must still honour the unsaved gate and `commit`.
- Hotkeys `R` and `C` mean different things per tab (Songs, Jam, coach); keep tab-aware mapping.

**Songs, library and stage**

- Portion selection by drag/shift-click/handles plus six hotkeys (`songs-loop-*`) and precise From/To fields: a path step must still allow choosing exact bars hands-free; keep `setEdge`/`nudgeRange` actions reachable from the path player.
- Per-song memory (selection, loop, speed, mixer, count-in, portions, drums choice) lives in `songMixes[songId]`: a path that auto-opens a song must restore it or pass explicit bars/tempo without overwriting it.
- Track (part) switching creates a separate record per part (own mix, takes, attempts); a front door that hides "which part am I on" will silently fragment history.
- Import entry points are five (picker, drop, OS file association, Downloads offer, library +); the Downloads offer only fires while the Songs tab is mounted, so a path-first home must keep Songs mounted or restart the watch.
- Band mixer, solo, drums kit/file choice and Settings sound font are the only way to silence the player's own part or fix a muddy mix; keep them within two taps.
- Count-in is the transport switch and is per song; speed steps 50-100% are the only slow-down tool.
- Starter shelf and "Included" fold state; deleting a starter must stay deleted.

**Songs, review**

- The review depends on the instrument input being on (`listening`); a one-tap "just play" front door that starts the transport without the input must still say a verdict will not appear, or silently produces no attempt/history. Preserve by keeping the listen gate (SNR-05) and surfacing it.
- Attempts are play-to-stop: a front door that restarts or re-pushes the schedule mid-pass (range/loop/tempo edits while playing) ends or corrupts the attempt. Preserve by keeping seek-not-recompile while playing (`seek_song`) and the 8-onset floor.
- The coach's four buttons carry state changes (selection, loop, tempo %, ramp climb, click subdivision, promise): hiding the review behind a summary card loses them. Preserve by keeping the pinned head with headline + button and the auto-climb on clean goes.
- "Come back" promises silently change how a song opens later (bars, loop, speed) and are never cleared (SNR-99); a learning-path front door must keep or replace the Due mark and the open-at-promised-bars behaviour.
- Camera/take are per-song opt-ins with a consent dialog and a promise text; one-tap record must still route through `request` doors (hotkeys R/C do), and camera on forces take on / take off forces camera off.
- Compare ("then and now") and takes-shelf facts rely on `attempts.take_path`; see Discrepancies (never written) before moving them into a path view.
- Save as video options (bars, band under picture, shape, marks, brand) and nudge are remembered settings; compress to one tap only if defaults stay `tab`, wide, marks on, brand on.
- Pitch read-back and per-note names only exist when a take with a dry stem exists; hiding the Record switch removes them.

**Coach**

- Listening only starts through Coach Start (COA-07, COA-61). A front door that scores playing must still call `start_evaluation` and `stop_evaluation`, stop the analyzer BEFORE reading `get_final_session_report` (`src/hooks/useSession.ts:1484`), and call `save_session`, or no segment closes and no history is written.
- Settings that live only in Settings > Coach: Brain tier, Scoring Mode, Coaching Style, Verbosity, Voice, Voice Name, Jam cues, Instrument (COA-88, COA-102 to COA-113). Keep them reachable; consider showing `coachMode` and `coachStance` where the score is shown, since they change grading and tone and are not visible in the panel.
- Brain download, Update brain, Remove models and the RAM gates are Settings and wizard-W4 only (COA-88 to COA-100). Any auto-start of the coach must still use the confirm dialog and the global progress/error bar.
- The `?` shortcut (pauses click, opens panel, focuses chat) and the History tab (detail, delete one, clear all, note-spacing trend) exist only inside the coach panel (COA-06, COA-44 to COA-48). They are the only per-session review surfaces besides the Metronome "Last session" line.
- Tip buttons (drop/bump 10 BPM, rest, posture, retry calibration) exist only as in-feed buttons with a cap of 2 per 5 minutes (COA-25, COA-26). Hiding the feed hides the only way to accept them.
- Spinner-until-voice and metronome ducking live in `tts_speak` / `speakAndReveal`; any new speaking surface must go through the same command to keep ducking, interruption and output-pair routing (COA-41, COA-106 to COA-108).
- Calibration cache, input device/channel and per-device input gain are shared with Devices settings (COA-62, COA-63, COA-116).
- Songs review uses coach blocks and verdicts, the practice chat uses chips and plain text (COA-37, COA-84 to COA-87). Merging them means keeping `useSongActions` wiring for loopBars, ramp, comeBack, clickSubdivision.

**Shell and system**

- Mode switch stops playback and a running ramp (SHL-04). A front door that jumps between tools (or into a learning-path step) must go through `setView` or replicate the `set_playing(false)` and `stop_speed_ramp` calls, or two modes will play at once.
- Settings is an overlay that remembers the covered mode (`prevTab`); a path-first shell needs the same "return to where you were" for Settings, the audio-error jump and the wizard/voice/theme detours (SHL-05, SHL-31, SHL-32).
- Zen is available from every mode but only knows three faces (`beat`/`drill`/`jam`); Setlist and Songs fall back to the metronome face (ZEN-18, ZEN-19). Keep Zen reachable from the rail, from Z and from double-click, and decide whether Songs/Setlist steps need their own Zen.
- Esc priority chain (tester > Zen > Settings; jam sheets) and typing guards (SHL-27, SHL-28) must survive any new full-screen surface.
- The floating widget is a second window with its own hotkey listener, theme, language, drag, position and size logic (WID-*). A lighter front door must keep `show_floating`/`show_main`, `lastWindow` and the tray "Show Yames" so the user can always get back.
- `app_ready` gating and position validation (SYS-16, SYS-17): the main window is created hidden; a new shell must still call `app_ready` once mounted or the window never appears.
- Close quits everything and finishes an active take (SYS-19); a shell with background activity must not bypass `CloseRequested`.
- Update banner exists only inside Settings (SYS-03); a one-tap UI that hides Settings hides updates, unless a prompt is added.
- Open-with from the OS goes through `take_pending_open` -> Songs importer and forces the Songs mode (SYS-07, SYS-08). A path-first front door must still route to Songs for an opened file.
- Persistent preferences that change feel (button flash, active border, drill auto-collapse, view animations, animation style, Zen style, language, widget mode) have only Settings entries; keep them reachable.

**Devices, onboarding, settings**

- Global shortcuts are advertised (defaults, column, plugin, capability) but never registered; a front door that promises system-wide control must either finish the registration (opt-in switch, per-OS combos) or keep the "soon" state honest.
- The Hotkeys table is the only place to remap keys, learn MIDI/pedal/gamepad, reset, and open the Input Tester. A simplified Settings must keep these four affordances reachable (`useMidi` learn, `actionForCombo` per-tab sharing, `footBindings`).
- Per-pair output routing (`audioOutputPairs`), channel selection (including interface loopback channels 3/4), per-device gain (`inputGain_<device>`) and the Recalibrate button live only in Settings > Devices and the Test modal; the wizard exposes none of them. Keep them reachable from a "my interface" shortcut.
- The audio-failure notice (`audio-error`) deep-links to `#settings-devices`; any restructure must preserve that anchor (also `#settings-coach`, `#settings-appearance`).
- Hint, tour and wizard state are separate stores (`hints.*`, `tour.seenVersion`, `onboarding.*`, `whatsNew.seenVersion`); a "reset onboarding" must clear all of them plus `appSessionCount`, `widgetOpened`, `hintSetupHistory` if it means "fresh install".
- Existing users are detected by `instrument` / sessions / presets. A learning-path front door that stops writing or changing `instrument` (or that migrates sessions/presets) can make an existing user look new and trigger the wizard over their data.
- `Test inputs` silences real actions while open; any new door must keep the MIDI/gamepad dispatch gates (`inputTestModeRef`, `onboarding.isOpen`).
- Wizard W6 is the only guided path that seeds `calibrationCache`; skipping it means the first real session pays the ~8-beat warmup.

## 4. Must not change

Changing any of these harms existing users' data or habits. If one has to change, it needs a migration and a test, as a stated decision.

### 4.1 Where everything lives

| Item | Value | If changed |
|---|---|---|
| App identifier | `com.yames.metronome` (src-tauri/tauri.conf.json:5) | Sets the app data directory. Renaming it orphans **all** user data. Keep it even if the product name changes |
| Settings store | `settings.json` via the store plugin, plus legacy `songs.json` | Everything in SK-* |
| Practice database | `practice.db`, `PRAGMA user_version` = 4 (src-tauri/src/db.rs:57,62). Refuses a file from a newer version and never migrates down | New migrations only, append-only; migrations 1 to 4 stay exactly as shipped. Any revamp build that bumps the schema locks users out of downgrading |
| Takes | `takes/<jam or song id>/` with WAV, JSON sidecar, video stem and thumbnail (src-tauri/src/take.rs:773,976) | Shelves go empty |
| Models | `models/brain/model.bin`, `tier`, `model.json`; `models/voice/` (src-tauri/src/models.rs) | Multi-GB re-download. Tier ids are frozen: `full` is Studio, `standard` |
| Session logs | `session_logs/` (src-tauri/src/session_log.rs:41) | Support tooling |
| Window labels | `main`, `floating` | Capabilities, `window_position_*`, routing via `?window=` |
| Update feed | `latest.json` on GitHub releases and the minisign `pubkey` (src-tauri/tauri.conf.json) | Existing installs stop updating, permanently |
| File associations | `gp`, `gp3`, `gp4`, `gp5`, `gpx`, `musicxml`, `mxl` | Guarded by src/tauriConfig.fileAssociations.test.ts |

### 4.2 Saved formats

| Record | Fields and frozen values | IDs |
|---|---|---|
| Preset (`presets`) | `id name createdAt bpm subdivision timeSignature beatGroups freeMode soundType volume view speedRamp`. `view` is `beat` or `drill`. `timeSignature: 0` is the retired Never accent and must still load | PRS-23 to PRS-26, SYS-29 |
| `speedRamp` | `startBpm targetBpm increment decrement barsPerStep beatsPerBar subdivision mode cyclic`; `mode` is `linear`, `zigzag` or `adaptive` | RMP-34 |
| Setlist (`setlists`) | `id name createdAt steps repeat countIn`; step `id name bpm subdivision beatGroups freeMode soundType volume trigger transition jamId`. Steps are copies, not pointers (U9.1). `chains` is still read as the legacy source | SET-01, SET-59, SYS-26 |
| Jam (`jams`) | Every field in `Jam` (src/jam/types.ts:403), including legacy `cues`; groove, vibe, kit and voice ids; built-in jam ids, because `lastJamId` and setlist `jamId` point at them | JAM-*, JTK-69 onward |
| Songs | `scores`, `attempts`, `attempt_onsets`, `attempt_extras`, `score_due` tables; `songMixes` keyed by song id; `songs.due`, `songs.dueTempo`; `songsStarterShelf` so a deleted starter stays deleted | SNG-*, SNR-* |
| History | `sessions`, `segments`, `drillRunHistory`, legacy `evalSessionHistory` import. Reserved tables `exercise_ceilings`, `routines`, `routine_runs`, `events` exist with no writers yet: a learning-path feature should fill them rather than add twins, and must not assume they hold data | COA-55 to COA-57, SYS-23 onward |
| Stored enums | Sound ids (click, sticks, wood, beep, drum, kit, snare, cowbell); theme ids (13); `animationStyle`; `coachBrainTier`; `coachMode` (`default`, `pro`); `coachVoiceMode` (`silent`, `voice`, a stored `chime` migrates); voices `lessac`, `amy`, `ryan`; locale codes (15); `activeTab` values `beat`, `setlist`, `drill`, `jam`, `songs`, where `beat` is the Metronome and unknown values are dropped silently | SHL-07, THM-01, I18-01 |

### 4.3 Settings keys

All keys in table SK (section 2.7.13). Read by Rust at startup, so renaming one on the TypeScript side alone silently resets the setting: `bpm subdivision mode corner alwaysOnTop widgetAlwaysOnTop accentColor theme volume soundType timeSignature beatGroups freeMode instrument speedRamp audioOutputDevice coachVoiceName coachTtsVolume midiBindings midiDevice lastWindow window_size_main window_position_main window_position_floating calibrationCache activeTab presets`.

Onboarding state keys (`onboarding.*`, `tour.seenVersion`, `whatsNew.seenVersion`, `hints.*`) decide what an existing user gets shown again. Keys prefixed `songs.camera*` and `songs.clip*` are app-wide despite the prefix (Jam reads them): do not "clean up" the names.

### 4.4 Hotkeys and actions

- **Action ids are the storage key** in `keyBindings`, `globalBindings`, `footBindings` and `midiBindings`. Renaming an id silently loses the user's keyboard, pedal and MIDI bindings, with no error. All 42 ids in table HK are frozen.
- **Defaults players have learned:** Space, Up, Down, Shift+Up, Shift+Down, `[`, `]`, `1` to `4`, `T`, Shift+T, `Z`, `F`, `W`, `B`, `C`, the jam keys `G X D S N L R`, the Songs keys.
- **`tab-1` to `tab-5` are positional and stored.** Metronome, Setlist, Drill, Jam, Songs. Adding Today to the rail must not renumber them: the last renumbering moved Drill and did not migrate stored bindings (SYS-33). Give Today a new action id, for example `tab-today`, and keep the five.
- **A changed default reaches only some users.** `{...defaults, ...stored}` means a user who has ever edited a binding has every id stored, so a new default reaches only people who never edited. New ids do reach everyone (KEY-06).
- **Per-tab sharing is by prefix.** `MODE_ACTION_PREFIXES` and the `view` name are matched in `actionForCombo`. A new view named differently from its action prefix breaks shared keys.
- **MIDI binding format:** `{action, channel, msgType: cc|note|pc, number}`.

### 4.5 Migrations that must stay in the code

`chains` to `setlists`; JSON history into SQLite; `songs.json` into SQLite (`movedToPracticeStore`); `songs.due` into SQLite (`songs.dueMovedToStore`); Never accent to `[4]`; `restore_beat_groups`; `chime` to silent; soon-instrument migration (src-tauri/src/lib.rs:347-363); legacy jam `cues` seeding `jamCues`. A user can upgrade from any old version, so none of these can be retired by the revamp.

## 5. Automated coverage and the smallest parity suite

### 5.1 IDs with no test at all

271 of 969 rows (28%) cite no test. 10 more are render-only. They are listed in the Test column. Rows were marked `none` when no test covers the behaviour, so a nearby test may exercise some of it indirectly. Rows stating an absence, or showing dead code, are included and do not need a test; they are SET-60, SET-65, SET-66, PRS-14, PRS-15, SNG-91, SNR-91, SNR-102, SNR-103, COA-70, COA-105, COA-119, AUD-25, SYS-09, CAL-07, CAL-08.

| SNR | 16 | 09, 23, 34, 62, 66, 72-73, 80, 82, 84, 87-88, 90-91, 102-103 |
| COA | 48 | 01-02, 04-05, 10-16, 18, 26, 28, 30-31, 36, 40-42, 44-46, 49, 60, 64, 67-70, 82, 87, 93, 98-99, 101-102, 104-105, 110-111, 114, 116-117, 119, 121-123 |
| PRS | 12 | 03, 07, 12-15, 17, 19, 26-27, 29, 31 |

| STG | 21 | 05-09, 11-13, 16-25, 27-29 |

A JAM count of 0 reflects that every JAM row cites a test, not always of the whole row. Coverage is thickest in Jam, Setlist and Songs logic and thinnest in **MIDI (13 of 18), Zen (14 of 22), widget (10 of 16), Settings (21 of 29), themes (7 of 10) and the Metronome stage controls**: the oldest and most used parts, and the ones the front door touches.

### 5.2 What already guards parity

| Guard | Protects | Limit |
|---|---|---|
| src/ipc.commands.test.ts | Everything invoked is registered in Rust | A removed feature passes |
| src/test/i18n.coverage.test.ts, src/test/i18n.locales.test.ts | No dead or missing string; 15 languages hold the same keys | **Deleting a feature together with its strings passes.** This was the revamp's main net last time and it does not catch deletion by itself |
| src/hooks/useActionDispatcher.jam.test.tsx, .songs.test.tsx; src/hotkeys.*.test.ts | Jam and Songs actions | Metronome, view and `tab-*` actions have no dispatcher test |
| src-tauri/src/db.rs tests | Migrations from v1 and v2 keep rows | Generated in code, no frozen file from a real release |
| src/containers/main-window/hooks/useTabRouting.test.ts, src/containers/main-window/Rail.test.tsx | Tabs and rail | Written for five modes |
| tests/layout/*.spec.ts | Things fit on screen, using the mock IPC | Fit, not behaviour |

### 5.3 Smallest parity suite

Eleven new test files. P1 to P4 are frozen lists and fixtures: cheap, about a day, and they catch the likeliest kind of loss (quiet deletion). P5 to P11 add behaviour where there is none today. Each of P5 to P11 is table-driven, one file, using the existing `src/test/mocks.ts` and `src/test/engineTicks.ts`.

| # | File (proposed) | Asserts | Guards |
|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | src/test/parity/surface.test.ts | Frozen lists, compared with the source: 144 Tauri commands, 29 events, 42 action ids with defaults, 5 play tabs, 13 theme ids, 8 sound ids, 15 locales, 9 hint ids. Removal needs an edit to the list, which review can see | Everything at the door, including the many `none` rows that hang off a command |
| P2 | src/test/parity/localeKeys.test.ts | A frozen snapshot of the 1,910 English keys, with an explicit deletion allowlist, as REVAMP_PARITY §2 intended. Fails when a key disappears | Every user-visible string: the cheapest whole-product net |
| P3 | src/test/parity/savedFormats.test.ts, with fixtures copied from v1.2.2 | Old preset (including `timeSignature: 0` and a drill preset), setlist, legacy `chains`, jam with `cues`, `songMixes`, `keyBindings`, all load through today's readers with no field lost | PRS-23 to PRS-26, SET-59, SYS-26, SYS-29, section 4.2 |
| P4 | Rust tests: `persist_state` round trip, plus a v4 `practice.db` fixture | Every key in 4.3 written and restored; a real v4 file opens with rows intact and no table dropped | MET-44, RMP-34, SYS-23 onward, STG-28 |
| P5 | src/hooks/useActionDispatcher.parity.test.tsx | The HK table run on every view: each of the 42 actions does its documented thing or is documented as ignored, including each future view | MET-08, MET-31, SET-40, SET-70, SHL-29, ZEN-21, HK-* |
| P6 | Rust unit tests in src-tauri/src/midi.rs, plus src/hooks/useMidi.test.ts and src/hooks/useKeybindings.test.ts | `parse_midi`, trigger rules (CC above 63, note velocity above 0), channel match, learn, conflict, exclusive binding; keymap merge, reset, conflict | MID-06 to MID-14, MID-16, KEY-03 to KEY-06, KEY-10, KEY-12 |
| P7 | src/containers/metronome/MetronomeView.parity.test.tsx, plus a `set_bpm` clamp test | Ruler, -/+, subdivision cards, clicks per bar, FREE, count-in switch, double-click to Zen; ramp defaults and popover collapse | MET-01 to MET-03, MET-12, MET-20, MET-21, MET-41, RMP-09, RMP-32, RMP-34 |
| P8 | src/containers/settings/settingsSurface.test.tsx | Table: each Settings control renders, writes its key, and reads back | STG-05 to STG-22, THM-04 to THM-09, SYS-01, SYS-18, COA-102, COA-110, COA-111 |
| P9 | src/containers/practice-coach/coachSurface.test.tsx | Open and close, chat send, history list, day grouping, delete one and all, empty state, remove models, chip states | COA-01, COA-02, COA-12, COA-16, COA-44 to COA-49, COA-98, COA-99 |
| P10 | src/takes/recordingDoors.test.tsx | Promise dialogs fire once, camera lifecycle, trouble chips, framing guide, save-as-video failure, reveal, recording through the door only | JTK-33 to JTK-37, JTK-41, JTK-42, JTK-52, SNR-72, SNR-73, SNR-84, SNR-87, SNR-88, SNR-90 |
| P11 | src/containers/shellExits.test.tsx | All seven Zen styles mount; widget keys and return to main; share targets and URLs; song import by picker and drop; download-offer settings; library export | ZEN-05 to ZEN-11, ZEN-19, WID-01, WID-02, WID-07, WID-13, SYS-12, SNG-01, SNG-02, SNG-18 to SNG-20, SNG-30, I18-03 |

**Plus the first revamp-specific test, once Today exists:** a one-tap Session on each tool writes the same `sessions`, `segments` and `attempts` rows as the same practice through the tool's own tab.

**Cannot be guarded by unit tests; keep them as a manual checklist.** Audio click timing (click-jitter probe), real MIDI and gamepad hardware, real camera and microphone, OS file open, tray, updater, window behaviour on each OS, and any coach model output. `plans/MANUAL_TEST_CHECKLIST.md` is the place. The note in REVAMP_PARITY §6 is relevant: setlist and engine behaviour was checked with a mock.

## 6. Discrepancies

### 6.1 Functional gaps that matter for parity

These are behaviours today that a parity test would pin down wrongly or that the revamp should decide on. **[V]** means re-checked by hand against the code; none has been run.

| # | Finding | IDs | Why it matters |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **[V] Global shortcuts do not exist.** The plugin, capabilities, default combos and `globalBindings` are all present, but nothing registers one. REVAMP_PARITY lists an opt-in for them, and the `@tauri-apps/plugin-global-shortcut` dependency suggests they work | KEY-11, WID-14, HK-01 to HK-05 | Parity means display only. Do not assume they work when designing hands-free flows |
| 2 | **[V] `attempts.take_path` is never written** (`attemptRecord` gets no `takePath`), so "then and now" compare and shelf facts probably never show on a real build | SNR-09 | Check on a real run before calling compare a feature to keep |
| 3 | **[V] `clear_due` has no production caller.** A song's promise stays due forever and keeps reopening that passage | SNR-96, SNR-99 | Interacts directly with a Today queue |
| 4 | **[V] Accent mode, drill aggressiveness and count-in are not persisted.** After every launch: "Group starts", moderate, count-in on with 4 beats | MET-44, RMP-34 | A user who turned the count-in off gets it back. Also Zen's drill start and preset load reset aggressiveness (not re-checked) |
| 5 | **[V] `exercise_ceilings`, `routines`, `routine_runs` have no writers**; the README's per-exercise progress over weeks is only partly true | COA-55 to COA-57 | The learning path may assume they hold data |
| 6 | **[V] Coach verbosity "Less" is lost on restart** (`useCoachDownload.ts` accepts only `default` and `more`) | COA-110 | Silent setting loss |
| 7 | **[V] The floating widget has no keyboard shortcuts on a fresh install.** It reads only stored `keyBindings`, and the main window writes those only after the user edits a binding | WID-13 | Reached only by keyboard users of the widget |
| 8 | **[V] The tour's Drill stop prints `tab-2`,** which is now Setlist | ONB-19 | Wrong shortcut shown |
| 9 | **[V] `tauri.windows.conf.json` replaces the `windows` array and omits `dragDropEnabled: false`.** If that replaces rather than merges, native drop may be on for Windows, which could affect DOM drag and drop there. The guard test reads only the base config. Not run | SYS-10, SNG-02, SET-31 | Check on Windows, the owner's platform |
| 10 | **[V] No `RunEvent::Opened` handler.** Double-clicking a score in macOS Finder may not reach the importer. Not run on macOS | SYS-07, SYS-08 | File association may be Windows and Linux only |
| 11 | MIDI hot-plug: re-plugging does not reconnect until restart. Bindings cannot be removed individually, because `clear_midi_binding` has no UI caller, and Reset to defaults leaves them. A note-on bound to an action also counts as a scored onset | MID-05, MID-13, MID-16 | Parity with a rough edge |
| 12 | Windows `open_url` runs `cmd /C start`, so `&` in the X and Reddit share links probably truncates them. Not run | SYS-12, SYS-13 | Share menu |
| 13 | Skip past a "When I say" setlist step has no hotkey, MIDI or pedal path, though `plans/UI_DECISIONS.md` U9.2 says "button, hotkey or footswitch" | SET-16, SET-49, SET-70 | Hands-free gap in a hands-free product |
| 14 | `toggle-sidebar` is ignored on Setlist, whose empty-state hint tells users to press it; `toggle-coach` is ignored on Setlist and Songs | HK-23, HK-24, COA-02 | Existing inconsistency, keep or fix on purpose |
| 15 | Zen on Setlist and Songs shows the metronome face; its BPM buttons call `set_bpm`, not the song tempo map. Not run | ZEN-18, ZEN-19 | A Session on a song needs a decision |
| 16 | Downloads offer and "turned down" hint pass `count` where the string expects `{{n}}`, so the number is probably blank | SNG-13, SNG-20 | Small |

Its **method** still holds, except the point noted in 5.2. Its **contents** are out of date, and the document describes v1.0.4.

### 6.2.1 Other plans

- `plans/ONBOARDING_PLAN.md` says six hints and a tour with its own keys. Code has nine hints, and `stops.ts` comments record that the plan's keys were wrong (P for presets, F for Zen).
- `plans/COACH_UX.md` is a draft "for the owner to react to", not a record of what shipped: do not read it as the coach's current behaviour. Section 2.6 is.

### 6.4 Oddities per area, as found

Code readings, not individually re-checked unless they appear above.

**Metronome and Ramp up**

- `accentMode`, drill `aggressiveness` and `warmupBeats` are never persisted (`persist_state` writes 9 speedRamp fields only; src-tauri/src/lib.rs restores the same 9). After every launch: accent resets to "Group starts", aggressiveness to moderate, and the count-in is ON with 4 beats (Rust default), so the first metronome Play via the button counts in even if the user turned it off last time.
- Zen's drill start (`handleRampToggle`) and preset load call `configure_speed_ramp` without `aggressiveness`, which Rust treats as moderate: a Push/Gentle choice is silently reset.
- The drill's Options count-in writes exactly 4 beats; the transport switch writes the metronome meter's bar length (up to 8), even while on the Drill tab (`countInToggle` ignores the drill's `beatsPerBar`). Two values for the same field.
- `drill.desc.countdown` says "4-beat countdown" but the stored value may be 1-8.
- Drill `beatsPerBar` allows 1 but the engine accents on a 4-beat bar when the value is below 2 (and Zen uses the same fallback); the UI draws 1 dot.
- Rust comment for drill subdivision says "1, 2, 3, 4 or 6" but the clamp and UI allow 1-6.
- `bpm-*` hotkeys on the Drill tab change `state.bpm` (metronome tempo) not the ramp's tempo; on Jam the dispatcher's copy does not update `jam.bpm` while the stage controls do.
- `sub-5`/`sub-6` hotkeys do not exist although the stage and cycle keys offer quintuplets and sextuplets; no tap-tempo hotkey or MIDI action exists.
- `GroupEditor` still declares `onBeatGroupsChange` and tests call it, but renders no editing UI (dots only; the group-resizing stepper was removed, tests named "reports the next bar through onBeatGroupsChange" and "wraps ... on the up stepper" actually drive `BeatStepper`).
- `src/hooks/useMetronome.ts` `DEFAULT_STATE` is stale versus Rust defaults (target 140 vs 120, bars per step 4 vs 12) but is replaced by `get_state` straight away.
- `onRampStep` is subscribed in DrillView with an empty handler ("for future use").
- `EvaluationPanel` is dead code (no importer), `src/containers/drill/lastRun.ts` computes underlay data the chart never draws, and `drill-view.css` still carries the comment for the underlay band.
- `set_active_tab`/`get_active_tab`, `set_always_on_top` etc. are outside this section; `start_playback`/`stop_playback` (src/ipc.ts:1479-1483) are audio-input commands, not the click.
- Mid-play meter change by hand restarts the bar immediately (not at the bar line); the "aligned on the bar" behaviour exists only for setlist step switches (`atBarLine`).
- Adaptive ignores `cyclic` and descending plans (it only steps up toward target or down to start).
- TS `SOUND_TYPES` has 8 entries and `INSTRUMENTS`/`SHARE_OPTIONS` also live in src/constants/metronome.ts but are other areas' data.

**Setlist and presets**

- `reorder_presets` (Rust) and `reorderPresets` (src/ipc.ts:406) are unused: the preset list is sorted by name at render (src/components/presets/PresetSidebar.tsx:354-358), not by stored order.
- `SetlistEmpty` component and locale keys `setlist.noneOpenTitle` / `noneOpenLead` are dead; the Setlist tab never shows an empty state (an empty setlist library renders a blank list).
- `setlistStepToPreset`, `stepConfigLabel` and `DEFAULT_TRIGGER` re-export have no production caller (tests only); `presetToSetlistStep` is only reached through "Add a step" on an empty setlist.
- Transition "Count me in" has no stepper in the UI (always 1 bar when chosen) though data and runtime accept any bar count (capped to 8 beats).
- `toggle-sidebar` is ignored on the Setlist tab (src/hooks/useActionDispatcher.ts:185) while the Setlist tab has a library with its own rail toggle.
- No "previous step", "restart" or "skip" hotkey / MIDI action exists; MIDI uses the same ids as hotkeys, so the setlist only gets `play`.
- Preset save skips ramp `aggressiveness` (type allows it); dirty check ignores it too.
- Preset "Update" from the context menu does not show the "Updated!" flash; only the save-bar path does.
- Setlist name limit is 20 (library rename) but step name limit is 24; preset name limit is 20.
- Setlists are stored only in `settings.json` from the frontend (store plugin); the legacy `chains` key is read once and left behind.

**Jam, music**

- Sections cannot be added, removed, renamed or repeated by the user (the request lists these; only the Song chorus count and the fixed AABA letters exist). Only way to get section structure beyond the five fixed forms is a pasted AABA chart.
- No solo and no master/click control for the band exists; the only per-player controls are volume (0-1.5) and an on/off switch.
- `ChordShapesRow` and `KeyChordsStrip` (`src/components/chords/*`, exported in `index.ts`) are not imported by any screen in this branch; only their own tests use them. The diatonic-chords strip in the brief is dead code; the cheat sheet's "In key" page covers the function.
- `jamNow` in `src/containers/main-window/hooks/useJamSession.ts:1432` has no UI caller; `vibeIsEdited` (`src/jam/vibes.ts:582`) is used only by tests (the "edited" word on the vibe chip is not wired); `jamFromChart` (`src/jam/chart.ts:497`) has no UI caller; `resizeGroove`, `resizePattern`, `meterCaption`, `cellLabel` in `src/containers/jam/editor/editorModel.ts` are exported but not used by the editor (the editor offers no way to change beats or subdivision of a drawn groove).
- `Jam.cues` is a legacy per-jam field; the spoken-cue switch is now the global `jamCues` setting. Old jams with `cues: true` are read once by `seedJamCues` (`src/containers/main-window/hooks/useUiPreferences.ts:85`) to seed the setting.
- `Jam.arrangement.intro` ("fill" / "none") has no control; only code and saved data set it.
- `jam.variation.yoursEmpty` and `jam.vibe.unavailable` strings: the first is unreferenced; the second only shows if `VIBES` were empty. `jam.perc.congas` / `jam.perc.bongos` are used dynamically.
- Kit lists differ in order: `JAM_KITS` (menu: club, studio, raw, tight, room, brushes, electronic) vs `JAM_KIT_IDS` (club, studio, raw, room, tight, brushes, electronic); same seven ids.
- `inspect_kit_folder` reports only the 11 drum voices; percussion samples in a custom folder are not looked for (percussion comes only from the shipped set). A legacy `snare_soft.wav` is still read.
- The vibe-picker uses the flat shape in `src/jam/vibesContract.ts` that wraps `src/jam/vibes.ts`; its header still calls itself a "stub until W19 lands" although it is wired; `applyIntensity` in the contract is a thin wrapper over `applyIntensityToGroove`.
- Hotkey `jam-take` and `jam-camera` share the `jam-` prefix and the same gating but belong to the takes area (not covered here). `src-tauri/src/synth.rs` is the song SoundFont synth, not used by the jam band (the jam band uses `src-tauri/src/kit.rs`, `src-tauri/src/jam.rs` and the recorded voices).
- Tempo clamp for the jam is applied in `MainWindow.handleBpmChange` (20-300), not in the jam module; `Jam.bpm` loaded from a saved record is not re-clamped on the TypeScript side.

**Jam, takes and library**

- Test names are stale: src/jam/jams.test.ts "opens on the six the first plan named" and src/containers/main-window/hooks/useJamSession.test.ts "seeds the six starters the first time" while `STARTER_JAMS` is 50 (6 hand-written + 44 `starter()`).
- `src/containers/jam/PracticeRow.tsx` no longer carries any take or camera control (only a comment says it left); the real switch is in JamSetupSheet. `JamView` receives `takes`/`camera` only to hand to the sheet.
- CameraPreview wrapper is `draggable` with a drag payload "camera" but no drop handler found in this area; only corner dots move it.
- Jam never uses `songs.clipBand` or the "Show the marks" control even though SaveAsVideo supports them.
- Many Jam-only camera/clip strings are `songs.*` locale keys (`songs.camera.*`, `songs.clip.*`) and the keys keep the `songs.` prefix (`songs.cameraIntroSeen` etc.) although they are app-wide.
- `jam.takes.*` strings are also used by src/containers/songs/SongTakes.tsx; `jam.takeSound.*` by both.
- `Jam.takes` and `Jam.camera` live in the jam record, so toggling them marks the jam "Edited" until saved, unlike the machine-wide `takes.soundSource`.
- No tests: useTakeCamera, CameraControl, CameraPreview, CameraIntroDialog, TakesIntroDialog, SaveAsVideo, JamTakeView behaviour (only layout screenshots), cheat-sheet pin/follow, jam context menu, Transport recording mark.
- `take_pending_open` and `analyze_take_pitch` appear in the take IPC list but belong to other areas (file open / Songs review), not covered here.

**Songs, library and stage**

- `songs.offer.more` ("{{n}} more waiting") and `settings.songs.turnedDownHint` ("{{n}} files...") use `{{n}}` but the code passes `count` (`src/containers/songs/DownloadOffer.tsx:42`, `src/containers/settings/SongsSettingsSection.tsx:170`), so the number is probably blank; same in es/fr/it.
- `song-dropped` is an engine "stream dropped" event (reload guard), not file drag-and-drop; the drop path is plain DOM `onDrop` (`dragDropEnabled:false`).
- Unused locale keys: `songs.exporting`, `songs.exportDone`, `songs.exportFailed` (export has no feedback), `songs.band.leftOut`, `songs.empty.body`, `songs.empty.private`, `songs.barsCount`, `src/test/i18n.songs-wave.test.ts` still lists them.
- Speed floor: session/saved-data clamp 50-100 (`src/containers/main-window/hooks/useSongsSession.ts:596`, `src/songs/songEngine.ts:312`) while types/engine allow 25-100 (`types.ts:322`, `src-tauri/src/song.rs:84`).
- Count-in UI only toggles 0/1 bar though engine, setter and saved data support 2.
- Picker/drop imports only the first dropped file; `.xml/.alphatex/.tex` import but are not OS file associations and never offered by the Downloads watch; `.mxl` untested.
- No "reopen last song" at startup: stage is empty until a row is clicked.
- Export of the stored file swallows errors silently (`src/components/presets/PresetSidebar.tsx:1439`).
- `plans/SONGS.md` A1-A4 are still marked "open" though built (engine plays the band, guide track, tempo map). A6's "let the existing speed ramp climb as passes are clean" is not implemented for songs: speed only changes by hand (speed ramp is stopped by `load_song`). Camera/video and "partnership catalogue" items are out of scope here.

**Songs, review**

- `attempts.take_path` is never written: `finishAttempt` calls `attemptRecord` with no `takePath` (`src/containers/songs/review/useSongAttempt.ts:268`) because the take is not named until after stop. Consequence: `useSongCompare.takeFor` and the takes-shelf "Bars X–Y · P% · goes · score" facts (`src/containers/songs/useSongTakes.ts:238`) never match, so the "then and now" block and the shelf facts appear dead in a real build (only in the shots mock). Worth a check against a real run.
- `clear_due` / `clearSongDue` has no caller outside tests; tests say "forgets one when it is played or dropped" but no code does. A promise keeps the song marked Due and re-opens the song on that passage forever.
- `useSongActions` returns `ramp` and `due`, neither read by SongsView (`actions.run` only); the ramp climb works internally with no visible status; `due` is only set after comeBack and never consumed.
- `songs.review.again` ("Go again") is unused; the button is "Back to the tab".
- `beatPositionBias`, `subdivisionWeak`, `drift` findings (39 sentences incl. these) can never appear: `analyze_free_play` has no non-test caller and is not a registered command.
- `src-tauri/src/srs.rs` (spaced repetition, 14-day cap) is not used for song promises; days are fixed by the finding (improved 2, clean 3, MAX_COME_BACK 14) despite `src-tauri/src/findings.rs` comments saying srs is "the real schedule".
- Free-play `FreePlayBeat`/`Fix` variants and coach actions `loadPreset`/`loadJam` are no-ops in the Songs review.
- Camera nudge key is per device only (no `sound` argument), so the separate "Everything this computer plays" nudge in `plans/SONGS.md` A12 applies to Jam, not Songs.
- Linux `reveal_in_folder` opens the parent folder, not the file.
- `plans/SONGS.md` unfinished/unverified, user-visible: A10/K3 camera latency has never been measured with a real camera (owner clap procedure; the nudge starts at 0 and the sidecar offset is a statistical fit with a known blind spot to constant delay; "noOffset" message shows when no fit); Linux system-sound loopback "compiled and fails safe rather than claimed", macOS not offered; A11 free-play refractory open (not Songs); A1 (what plays the backing) and several A-entries still marked open/for the owner to confirm (A8 dry stem, A10 picture file). T-A 1.2 real 180 BPM capture marked as owner-played, pending.
- Hit window/colour bands come from `src-tauri/src/timing.rs` tempo-aware thresholds; band values are not user adjustable anywhere.

**Coach**

- README "audio notifications (chime or natural voice)": FALSE today. Chime was removed; `coachVoiceMode` is only `silent` or `voice` and a stored `chime` is migrated to silent (`src/hooks/useCoachDownload.ts:135`).
- README "comments appear in a chat-like feed": TRUE (COA-19, COA-118).
- README "Automatic detection (grid exercise vs free playing)": TRUE (playMode structured/noodling, Grid exercise / Semi-structured / Free playing; COA-12, COA-34, COA-39).
- README "Session reports ... timeline, natural-language summaries": TRUE (COA-38, COA-39, COA-76). The summary sentence comes from the model only when one is resident, otherwise from Rust templates.
- README "Adaptive drill mode ... pushes / backs off": TRUE (Drill tab Adaptive mode, `adaptive-eval` from `src-tauri/src/engine.rs:8364`, coach narrates; COA-33). The same README says Adaptive is "coming soon" in the Speed Drill section, which is stale.
- README "Preset history ... comfortable BPM and accuracy over weeks and months": PARTLY TRUE. History is stored and the coach reads it (greeting, ceiling, stamina, chat; COA-55, COA-56) and the Drill tab keeps `drillRunHistory`, but there is no per-preset progress chart in the coach panel and the `exercise_ceilings` table (comfortable_bpm, peak_bpm, SRS fields) is never written (COA-57).
- README "Conversational" and "Fully local": TRUE (chat answers from the local model or the Rust phrase bank; downloads are the only network use).
- README `C` toggles the coach: TRUE on Metronome, Drill, Jam only. On Setlist the panel renders but the key does nothing; on Songs there is no panel (COA-02, COA-05).
- `coachVerbosity` "less" can be chosen in Settings but is not restored after a restart: `src/hooks/useCoachDownload.ts:153` accepts only "default" or "more" (COA-110).
- Status chip states "Thinking…" and "Speaking…" can never show: `src/containers/main-window/MainWindow.tsx:2208` does not pass `coachLoading` or `ttsActive` (COA-119).
- `evaluationShowRealtime` is saved and loaded, but `toggleRealtime` / `showRealtime` have no UI and no reader (COA-64).
- `onset-detected` is emitted from `src-tauri/src/commands.rs` (audio and MIDI paths) with no listener in `src/` (COA-70).
- Registered commands never called from the frontend: `list_session_logs`, `get_session_log`, `clear_session_logs`, `start_voice_repair`, `get_models_path`, `write_model_chunk` (ipc wrappers `listSessionLogs`, `getSessionLog`, `clearSessionLogs`, `startVoiceRepair`, `getModelsPath`, `writeModelChunk`, `downloadModelFile` unused) (COA-81, COA-105).
- Locale tooltips `voiceEngineMissing`, `voiceCorrupted`, `voiceFilesMissing` say "click Repair below" but no per-voice Repair button exists; the single "Download voices" button repairs (COA-104).
- The `take-break` affordance (rest 30 s, posture 60 s) only hides its buttons; there is no rest timer (comment in `src/hooks/useSession.ts` says deferred).
- `src/containers/drill/EvaluationPanel.tsx` is dead code with a live test; its `eval.*` locale keys have no other user (COA-120).
- `src/coach/blocks/actions.ts` (`runCoachAction`, `useCoachAction`) is used only by tests and the gallery; Songs wires its own `useSongActions`. Blocks are never rendered in the practice chat (COA-84, COA-86).
- Verdicts are text only; no code path speaks them. `ttsSpeak` callers are `src/hooks/useSession.ts`, `src/containers/settings/CoachSettingsSection.tsx` and `src/containers/main-window/hooks/useJamSession.ts` only.
- `src-tauri/src/voices.rs` is the Jam melodic sample banks, not coach voices. Coach speech is `src-tauri/src/tts.rs` + `src-tauri/src/speech_out.rs`; exactly 3 coach voices (lessac, amy, ryan; `src-tauri/src/tts.rs:35`).
- Locale `brainStandardNeedsRam` / `brainStudioNeedsRam` and `coachDownload.studioNeedsRam` say 8 GB / 16 GB; the Rust gates are 7.5 GiB / 15 GiB (`src-tauri/src/models.rs:64-68`).
- `HistoryFilter.exercise_key` and the `sessions` columns `exercise_key`, `log_path`, `narrative` are never filled, and `events` (coach utterances) is never written.
- The doc comment on `ScoreWeights::default` says 0.40/0.20/0.25/0.15, but every shipped instrument profile has `oe` 0.00 and different IC/GA weights (COA-72).
- `src/hooks/useSession.ts` (2478 lines) holds greeting, gatekeeper loop, settings-boundary, grid-discontinuity, adaptive narration, chat, chips and save; only `useRealtimeTips` and `useSegmentCoach` are split out.
- `notify_settings_change` is called only from `src/hooks/useSession.ts:1100`, so tempo or meter edits outside an active coach session never close a segment (nothing is listening then anyway).

**Shell and system**

- `fullscreen-changed` event: listened to in `src/containers/main-window/hooks/useFullscreenLifecycle.ts:74` and typed in `src/ipc.ts:198`, but nothing in src-tauri/src emits it (grep: no match). Dead listener; Zen state only changes through React state.
- Global (OS-wide) shortcuts do not exist in practice: `@tauri-apps/plugin-global-shortcut` and `tauri-plugin-global-shortcut` are dependencies and `global-shortcut:*` capabilities are granted, but no JS or Rust code registers a shortcut or calls `.plugin(...global_shortcut...)`. `globalKey` ⌘⇧O and `globalBindings` are stored/displayed only.
- Widget keyboard shortcuts read only the stored `keyBindings` key (`src/containers/floating-widget/FloatingWidget.tsx:127`). The main window writes that key only after the user edits a binding, so on a fresh install the widget has no active shortcuts (inferred from `src/hooks/useKeybindings.ts:60-77`; not run).
- Widget ignores `sub-1..sub-4`, tab, jam and songs actions; `toggle-widget` and `os-fullscreen` both mean "back to main" there.
- `corner` and `accentColor` (and `widgetAlwaysOnTop`-style persistence for them) are saved/loaded but have no setter command and no UI; `AppState.corner/accentColor` are vestigial.
- Tray: `tauri.conf.json` declares `trayIcon` AND `src-tauri/src/lib.rs:644` builds a second tray with no explicit icon; tray "Show Yames" does not write `lastWindow` or unminimise (unlike `show_main`), so the next launch can still open the widget. There is no left-click handler.
- Single-instance: a second launch with no openable file does nothing (no focus of the running window) because `announce_opened_paths` returns when `queued == 0`.
- macOS Finder file-open: only argv and the single-instance callback are handled; there is no `RunEvent::Opened` handler in src-tauri/src/lib.rs, so double-clicking a `.gp5` on macOS may not reach `take_pending_open` (not run; Tauri delivers macOS opens as `RunEvent::Opened`).
- `open_url` on Windows runs `cmd /C start <url>`; share URLs for X and Reddit contain `&`, which `cmd` treats as a command separator (not run; likely truncates the link). `open_url` also does no scheme check.
- `tauri.windows.conf.json` replaces the whole `windows` array and omits `dragDropEnabled:false`; `src/tauriConfig.dragDrop.test.ts` only reads the base `tauri.conf.json`, so the Windows override may re-enable native file drop (merge semantics not run).
- Hotkey scope gaps: `toggle-sidebar` is ignored on the Setlist view, `toggle-coach` is ignored on Setlist and Songs (`src/hooks/useActionDispatcher.ts:184-191`), even though the rail shows both controls there.
- The Rail Zen button works on every mode, the double-click Zen shortcut only on Metronome and Drill (`src/containers/main-window/MainWindow.tsx:1716`).
- Zen on Songs/Setlist shows the metronome face and its BPM buttons call `set_bpm`, not the song tempo map (not run; interaction with the song engine unverified).
- `song-dropped` is an engine-unloaded-the-song event, not a drag-and-drop file event; files dropped on the window are ignored by design (SYS-10).
- Sidebar open state (`sidebarOpen`) is not persisted; the Settings sheet position, Zen state and widget corner are also not restored.
- Language change is applied in the main window only; the already-running widget window does not hear it until restart (widget reads `language` once at mount in `src/App.tsx:25`). Share option names (WhatsApp, X / Twitter, Facebook, Reddit) are not localised.
- Tab-number meaning changed (Drill moved ⌘2 -> ⌘3) with no migration of stored bindings (SYS-33).
- Many shell pieces have no test: `useAppUpdates`, `useFullscreenLifecycle`, `useShareMenu`, `useDownbeatPulse`, `ThemeEffects`, `ViewTransition`, `ZenTransition`, all six Zen effects, `WindowControls`, `SystemStatusChip`, `useGamepad`, tray, window persistence, single-instance, deep-link-style scrolls.
- `fs-exit-hint`/`zen.exitHint` is shown even on touch-less cases; `Escape` handler in `FullscreenView` is also registered at capture phase while MainWindow has a second Escape handler for Zen (`src/containers/main-window/MainWindow.tsx:1410`), so two paths close Zen (harmless double call).

**Devices, onboarding, settings**

- Global shortcut column is dead code: `globalBindings` are stored and shown but no `register()` exists in Rust or JS; capture type "global" cannot be started (buttons are `disabled`); `plugin-global-shortcut` is only a dependency/capability.
- `SpectrumAnalyzer` (`src/components/SpectrumAnalyzer.tsx`) and `InstrumentPickerModal` (`src/components/InstrumentPickerModal.tsx:53`) have no mount; only `InstrumentPickerGrid` is used (wizard W1).
- `set_calibration_offset` / `get_calibration_offset` / `list_calibration_cache` wrappers have no UI caller; `calibrationOffset` is write-only/legacy.
- `onboarding.skipped` is saved but never loaded. `hints` label "six one-time tips" and Reset text are stale (9 hints, none of the hint side-state is reset).
- `clear_midi_binding` / `useMidi.removeBinding` are never called from the UI; MIDI bindings cannot be removed individually and "Reset to defaults" leaves them (it only resets key, global and foot maps), although the confirm dialog says "all keyboard bindings".
- The MIDI conflict "Don't ask again" box is React state (`midiAutoAccept`), reset on every launch. There is no conflict check between a MIDI/gamepad learn and keyboard bindings, nor between MIDI learn and foot (gamepad) bindings.
- Hotkey conflict detection flags any identical combo, including the intentional tab-scoped sharing (R, C, L, `[`, `]`), and "Reset to default" for one action does no conflict check.
- MIDI hot-plug: unplug closes the connection but re-plug never reconnects (only app restart does). The backend polls every 3 s (MIDI) and every 5 s on device COUNT (audio); an input device that disappears while selected is not cleared (output is).
- Every MIDI note-on counts as an evaluation onset even if it is bound to an action (a Note-bound pedal pollutes a scored session). MIDI learn accepts the first activity of any value, so a pedal release (CC 0) can become the binding even though it would never trigger (needs value > 63).
- Wizard W1 offers drums and piano; the Settings Instrument dropdown disables them ("soon"). The tour's Drill stop prints key `tab-2` (`src/containers/onboarding/tour/stops.ts`) but `tab-2` is now Setlist (Drill is `tab-3`), so the card shows the wrong shortcut.
- Settings timeline has 8 ticks for 9 sections (no Songs tick), so ticks from Widget onwards are off by one against the real section order.
- `midi-plugged` hint action scrolls to `section.hotkeys-section`, which first matches Devices (Devices also uses that class).
- `settings.songs.turnedDownHint` expects `{{n}}` but code passes `count`; `settings.tabs.*` strings exist only for the timeline.
- Calibration cache key: UI passes `null`/"" for system default, backend maps `None` to "default"; an explicit empty string device name is not the same key (unverified whether `selectDevice("")` produces a different key).
- `useAudioOutputDevices` and Settings treat "System default" as "" while the backend stores `audioOutputDevice` as null; `audioOutputPairs` uses "" as its key.

## Appendix B. Supporting source files with no row of their own

The file check found 39 non-test source files that no row cites. None holds a separate user capability, except the two dead chord components noted.

| Files | Serves |
|---|---|
| src-tauri/src/onset.rs, src-tauri/src/pitch.rs | Onset detection and pitch tracking behind COA and SNR rows |
| src-tauri/src/session_audio.rs | Raw session audio dump, debug builds only |
| src-tauri/src/alloc_probe.rs, src-tauri/src/clock.rs, src-tauri/src/main.rs, src/main.tsx, src/vite-env.d.ts | Plumbing |
| src/coach/blocks/slots.tsx, src/coach/blocks/types.ts, src/containers/onboarding/steps/types.ts | Types and slots for COA and ONB rows |
| src/components/AppMark.tsx | App mark in the title bar (SHL) |
| src/components/chords/ChordDiagram.tsx, src/components/fretboard/Fretboard.tsx | Drawing for the cheat sheet (JTK-57 onward) and coach blocks |
| src/components/chords/ChordShapesRow.tsx, src/components/chords/KeyChordsStrip.tsx | **Not mounted by any screen**; tests only (see 6.4) |
| src/containers/drill/evaluation/{BreakdownBar,Histogram,MiniSparkline,ScoreBadge,ScoreRing}.tsx, src/containers/drill/evaluation/helpers.ts | Used by coach history and detail and by the wizard's hear-it-work step, despite the folder name. Must survive if `EvaluationPanel` is deleted |
| src/containers/jam/JamSelect.tsx, src/containers/jam/Segmented.tsx, src/containers/jam/useMenuPlacement.ts | Shared controls; also used by Songs and takes |
| src/containers/onboarding/anchor.ts, src/containers/onboarding/hints/useFirstTimeHint.ts | Tour and hint placement (ONB) |
| src/containers/settings/formatBytes.ts, src/containers/zen/effects/helpers.ts | Helpers |
| src/containers/songs/review/reviewFixtures.ts, src/songs/fixtures.ts | Test and screenshot fixtures |
| src/containers/songs/tabGroups.ts | Maps schedule onsets to engraved notes for the stage (SNG, SNR live note lights) |
| src/jam/bandChord.ts, src/jam/bassline.ts, src/jam/keysline.ts, src/jam/diatonic.ts | Band line generation and key logic behind JAM rows |
| src/takes/clipStrip.ts, src/takes/src.ts, src/takes/tabTape.ts | Video export internals (JTK-48, SNR-67) |
